```shellscript
#!/bin/bash

# COMPASS Writing Guides MCP - Setup Script
# Comprehensive fiction writing reference database

set -e  # Exit on error

echo "=========================================="
echo "COMPASS Writing Guides MCP Setup"
echo "=========================================="
echo ""

# Get the directory where this script is located
SCRIPT_DIR="$( cd "$( dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}" )" && pwd )"
cd "$SCRIPT_DIR"

echo "Working directory: $SCRIPT_DIR"
echo ""

# Check if data file exists
echo "Checking data file..."
if [ ! -f "data/writing_guides.json" ]; then
    echo "ERROR: data/writing_guides.json not found!"
    exit 1
fi
echo "✓ Found data/writing_guides.json"
echo ""

# Check Python 3 installation
echo "Checking Python installation..."
if ! command -v python3 &> /dev/null; then
    echo "ERROR: Python 3 is not installed."
    echo "Please install Python 3 and try again."
    exit 1
fi

PYTHON_VERSION=$(python3 --version)
echo "✓ Found: $PYTHON_VERSION"
echo ""

# Create virtual environment
echo "Creating virtual environment..."
if [ -d "venv" ]; then
    echo "Virtual environment already exists. Removing old one..."
    rm -rf venv
fi

python3 -m venv venv
echo "✓ Virtual environment created"
echo ""

# Activate virtual environment
echo "Activating virtual environment..."
source venv/bin/activate
echo "✓ Virtual environment activated"
echo ""

# Install dependencies
echo "Installing MCP package..."
pip install --quiet --upgrade pip
pip install --quiet mcp
echo "✓ MCP package installed"
echo ""

# Make server executable
echo "Making server.py executable..."
chmod +x server.py
echo "✓ server.py is now executable"
echo ""

# Get full path to Python in venv
VENV_PYTHON="$SCRIPT_DIR/venv/bin/python"
SERVER_PATH="$SCRIPT_DIR/server.py"

echo "=========================================="
echo "Setup Complete!"
echo "=========================================="
echo ""
echo "Next steps:"
echo ""
echo "1. Add this to your Cursor MCP settings:"
echo "   Location: ~/.cursor/mcp.json (or .cursor/config/mcp.json)"
echo ""
echo '   {'
echo '     "mcpServers": {'
echo '       "compass-writing-guides": {'
echo "         \"command\": \"$VENV_PYTHON\","
echo '         "args": ['
echo "           \"$SERVER_PATH\""
echo '         ]'
echo '       }'
echo '     }'
echo '   }'
echo ""
echo "2. Completely quit and restart Cursor"
echo ""
echo "3. Test with this prompt:"
echo "   'Use compass-writing-guides MCP to get Stephen King's advice on adverbs'"
echo ""
echo "Available queries:"
echo "  - Get author advice (Stephen King, Anne Lamott, etc.)"
echo "  - Get topic guidance (dialogue, pacing, character, etc.)"
echo "  - Get author style guides"
echo "  - Get writing quotes"
echo "  - Get techniques"
echo "  - Get genre guidance"
echo "  - Search across all guides"
echo ""
echo "=========================================="
echo ""
echo "Setup script finished!"
echo ""
echo "Read README.md for detailed usage instructions."

```

Creating the README documentation:


```markdown
# COMPASS Writing Guides MCP

**Comprehensive Fiction Writing Reference Database**

A Model Context Protocol (MCP) server providing instant access to writing advice, author insights, techniques, and principles from master writers. This is the **first-of-its-kind Writing Guides MCP** for fiction writers.

---

## 🎯 What This MCP Provides

### **Author Writing Advice**
- **Stephen King** - "On Writing" principles and quotes
- **Anne Lamott** - "Bird by Bird" insights
- **Brandon Sanderson** - Craft-focused advice
- More authors can be added easily

### **Writing Topics & Principles**
- Dialogue
- Pacing
- Character Development
- Prose Style
- Atmosphere & Mood
- Point of View
- Show Don't Tell
- Revision

### **Author Style Guides**
Complete style breakdowns for:
- Stephen King
- Richard Russo
- Dennis Lehane
- Donna Tartt
- J.D. Salinger
- Annie Proulx
- Cormac McCarthy
- Colson Whitehead

### **Writing Techniques**
Organized techniques for:
- Pacing techniques
- Dialogue techniques
- Character techniques
- Prose techniques
- Atmosphere techniques

### **Genre Guidance**
Specific advice for:
- Literary Fiction
- Crime/Mystery
- Horror/Supernatural
- Coming of Age

### **Writing Quotes**
Inspirational quotes from master writers, searchable by author or keyword.

---

## 🚀 Quick Start

### 1. Setup

```bash
cd zz_COMPASS/MCP_Implementations/COMPASS_Writing_Guides_MCP
./setup.sh
```

This will:
- Create a Python virtual environment
- Install MCP dependencies
- Make server.py executable

### 2. Configure Cursor

Add to `~/.cursor/mcp.json` (or `.cursor/config/mcp.json`):

```json
{
  "mcpServers": {
    "compass-writing-guides": {
      "command": "/full/path/to/COMPASS_Writing_Guides_MCP/venv/bin/python",
      "args": [
        "/full/path/to/COMPASS_Writing_Guides_MCP/server.py"
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

**Note:** Replace `/full/path/to/` with the actual absolute path to your project directory.

### 3. Restart Cursor

**Important:** Completely quit Cursor (Cmd+Q) and restart for MCP changes to take effect.

### 4. Test It

Try these queries:

```
Use compass-writing-guides MCP to get Stephen King's advice on adverbs
```

```
Use compass-writing-guides MCP to get guidance on dialogue
```

```
Use compass-writing-guides MCP to get Richard Russo's style guide
```

---

## 📚 Available Tools

### **Get Author Advice**
```
Get writing advice and principles from a specific author
```
**Parameters:**
- `author` (required): Author name (e.g., "Stephen King", "Anne Lamott")
- `topic` (optional): Filter by topic (e.g., "adverbs", "dialogue")

**Example:**
```
Use compass-writing-guides MCP to get Stephen King's advice on dialogue
```

### **Get Topic Guidance**
```
Get comprehensive guidance on a writing topic
```
**Parameters:**
- `topic` (required): Topic name (e.g., "dialogue", "pacing", "character_development", "prose_style", "atmosphere", "point_of_view", "show_dont_tell", "revision")

**Example:**
```
Use compass-writing-guides MCP to get guidance on pacing
```

### **Get Author Style**
```
Get detailed author style guide (prose style, narrative technique, themes, signature moves, use cases)
```
**Parameters:**
- `author` (required): Author name

**Available Authors:**
- Stephen King
- Richard Russo
- Dennis Lehane
- Donna Tartt
- J.D. Salinger
- Annie Proulx
- Cormac McCarthy
- Colson Whitehead

**Example:**
```
Use compass-writing-guides MCP to get Donna Tartt's style guide
```

### **Get Quote**
```
Get inspirational writing quotes
```
**Parameters:**
- `author` (optional): Filter by author name
- `keyword` (optional): Search for quotes containing keyword

**Example:**
```
Use compass-writing-guides MCP to get quotes about revision
```

### **Get Technique**
```
Get techniques for a specific aspect of writing
```
**Parameters:**
- `technique_type` (required): Type of technique (e.g., "pacing_techniques", "dialogue_techniques", "character_techniques", "prose_techniques", "atmosphere_techniques")

**Example:**
```
Use compass-writing-guides MCP to get dialogue techniques
```

### **Get Genre Guidance**
```
Get genre-specific writing guidance
```
**Parameters:**
- `genre` (required): Genre name (e.g., "literary_fiction", "crime_mystery", "horror_supernatural", "coming_of_age")

**Example:**
```
Use compass-writing-guides MCP to get guidance for crime mystery
```

### **Search Guides**
```
Search across all writing guides by keyword
```
**Parameters:**
- `query` (required): Search keyword or phrase

**Example:**
```
Use compass-writing-guides MCP to search for advice on character motivation
```

### **List Authors**
```
List all available authors with writing advice
```

### **List Topics**
```
List all available writing topics
```

### **Get Writing Tip**
```
Get a quick writing tip on a specific topic or from a specific author
```
**Parameters:**
- `topic` (optional): Topic name
- `author` (optional): Author name

**Example:**
```
Use compass-writing-guides MCP to get a writing tip on dialogue from Stephen King
```

---

## 💡 Usage Examples

### During Chapter Writing

**Before writing dialogue:**
```
Use compass-writing-guides MCP to get guidance on dialogue, then help me write authentic dialogue for this scene between Nick and Jackie.
```

**Stuck on pacing:**
```
Use compass-writing-guides MCP to get pacing techniques, then help me improve the pacing of this action scene.
```

**Character development:**
```
Use compass-writing-guides MCP to get guidance on character development, then help me deepen Maxanne's emotional journey in this chapter.
```

### Style Reference

**Adopting an author's voice:**
```
Use compass-writing-guides MCP to get Richard Russo's style guide, then help me rewrite this scene with Russo's working-class empathy and community focus.
```

**Multiple style references:**
```
Use compass-writing-guides MCP to get Dennis Lehane's style guide and Stephen King's style guide. I want to combine Lehane's Boston grit with King's accessible prose for this scene.
```

### Revision & Editing

**Prose improvement:**
```
Use compass-writing-guides MCP to get Stephen King's advice on adverbs, then help me identify and fix adverb overuse in this passage.
```

**Show don't tell:**
```
Use compass-writing-guides MCP to get guidance on show_dont_tell, then help me rewrite these paragraphs to show emotions instead of telling them.
```

### Inspiration & Learning

**Quick tips:**
```
Use compass-writing-guides MCP to get a writing tip on character development
```

**Quotes for motivation:**
```
Use compass-writing-guides MCP to get quotes from Stephen King about writing practice
```

**Genre guidance:**
```
Use compass-writing-guides MCP to get guidance for literary_fiction, then help me understand if this chapter fits the genre conventions.
```

---

## 🔧 Data Structure

The MCP uses a JSON database (`data/writing_guides.json`) organized into:

- **`authors`**: Writing advice and principles from specific authors
- **`topics`**: Comprehensive guidance on writing topics
- **`author_styles`**: Detailed author style guides
- **`techniques`**: Organized writing techniques
- **`genre_guidance`**: Genre-specific advice
- **`quotes`**: Inspirational writing quotes

---

## 📝 Adding New Content

The data file (`data/writing_guides.json`) is human-readable JSON. You can:

1. **Add new authors**: Add entries to `authors` and/or `author_styles`
2. **Add new topics**: Add entries to `topics`
3. **Add new quotes**: Add to `quotes.general_writing` or create author-specific quote sections
4. **Add techniques**: Add to `techniques`
5. **Add genres**: Add to `genre_guidance`

**No code changes needed** - just edit the JSON file and the MCP will use the new content immediately (after a Cursor restart if the server is running).

---

## 🔗 Integration with COMPASS

This MCP integrates seamlessly with the COMPASS Writing System:

- **Works with COMPASS Agents**: Agents can query this MCP for writing guidance
- **Complements Character MCP**: Character MCP handles character data; Writing Guides MCP handles writing craft
- **References Author Style Library**: Uses content from `Story_Bible/author-style-library.md`
- **Uses Commands Content**: Incorporates principles from `zz_COMPASS/Commands/`

---

## 🎨 Example Workflows

### Workflow 1: Writing a Dialogue Scene

1. Query: `Get guidance on dialogue`
2. Query: `Get Stephen King's advice on dialogue`
3. Write the scene using the guidance
4. Query: `Get techniques for dialogue`
5. Refine the scene using specific techniques

### Workflow 2: Adopting an Author's Style

1. Query: `Get Richard Russo's style guide`
2. Review prose style, narrative technique, themes
3. Query: `Get Russo's signature moves`
4. Apply the style to your scene
5. Query: `Get guidance on show_dont_tell` (to ensure authentic application)

### Workflow 3: Genre-Specific Writing

1. Query: `Get guidance for literary_fiction`
2. Review focus, pacing, key elements
3. Query: `Get representative authors` (to see examples)
4. Query: `Get [author]'s style guide` for reference
5. Write with genre conventions in mind

---

## 🚨 Troubleshooting

### "MCP server not found"
- Verify the path in `mcp.json` is absolute (not relative)
- Ensure you've restarted Cursor completely (Cmd+Q, then reopen)
- Check that `server.py` is executable: `chmod +x server.py`

### "Author not found"
- Check available authors: `Use compass-writing-guides MCP to list authors`
- Author names are case-sensitive (e.g., "Stephen King" not "stephen king")

### "Topic not found"
- Check available topics: `Use compass-writing-guides MCP to list topics`
- Topic names use underscores (e.g., "character_development" not "character development")

### Server errors
- Check that virtual environment is activated
- Verify MCP package is installed: `pip list | grep mcp`
- Check server.py is executable and has correct shebang: `#!/usr/bin/env python3`

## 🚀 Next Steps

### 1. Run Setup Script

```bash
cd zz_COMPASS/MCP_Implementations/COMPASS_Writing_Guides_MCP
./setup.sh
```

This creates the virtual environment and installs dependencies.

### 2. Add to MCP Configuration

Add to `~/.cursor/mcp.json` (or `.cursor/config/mcp.json`):

```json
{
  "mcpServers": {
    "compass-writing-guides": {
      "command": "/Users/timothy/Library/Mobile Documents/com~apple~CloudDocs/Cursor/03_Novels/Walk This Way/zz_COMPASS/MCP_Implementations/COMPASS_Writing_Guides_MCP/venv/bin/python",
      "args": [
        "/Users/timothy/Library/Mobile Documents/com~apple~CloudDocs/Cursor/03_Novels/Walk This Way/zz_COMPASS/MCP_Implementations/COMPASS_Writing_Guides_MCP/server.py"
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

**Note:** Use the absolute path shown above (your iCloud location).

### 3. Restart Cursor

**Important:** Completely quit Cursor (Cmd+Q) and restart for MCP to load.

### 4. Test It!

Try these queries:

```
Use compass-writing-guides MCP to get Stephen King's advice on adverbs
```

```
Use compass-writing-guides MCP to get guidance on dialogue
```

```
Use compass-writing-guides MCP to get Richard Russo's style guide
```

```
Use compass-writing-guides MCP to search for advice on character motivation
```

    "scalpel_hose": {
      "name": "Scalpel & Hose Philosophy",
      "subtitle": "The Blood Brothers System for Novel Writing",
      "source": "Agent_Work/SCALPEL_HOSE_PHILOSOPHY.md",
      "description": "A dual methodology for editing and enhancing prose: Scalpel cuts with precision, Hose adds depth with specificity",
      "core_principle": "Cut 50% + Add Depth = Exponential Quality Improvement",
      "scalpel": {
        "mission": "Cut with precision, eliminate waste, preserve what matters",
        "cuts": [
          "Purple prose and overwrought metaphors",
          "Repetitive phrases and clichéd language",
          "Abstract philosophy and over-explaining",
          "Section headers that break narrative flow",
          "Redundant descriptions and telling what we just showed",
          "Characters who are cardboard cutouts or clichés",
          "Prose that sounds 'literary' rather than authentic"
        ],
        "tools": [
          "Brutal honesty about what doesn't work",
          "Pattern recognition across chapters",
          "Timeline consistency enforcement",
          "Character voice accuracy",
          "Trust the reader principle"
        ]
      },
      "hose": {
        "mission": "Add depth, create meaning, earn emotion through specificity",
        "adds": [
          "Character motivations and the COST of their choices",
          "Specific fears (not abstract worries)",
          "Internal moments showing WHY characters choose what they choose",
          "Concrete sensory details that carry emotion",
          "One 'defining moment' for each supporting character",
          "Money sentences that hit hard and earn their place"
        ],
        "core_question": "What does this choice COST this character? What are they giving up?"
      },
      "character_depth_hierarchy": {
        "tier_1_main_characters": {
          "examples": ["Protagonist", "Antagonist", "Love Interest", "Mentor", "Primary Family Members"],
          "requirements": [
            "ALWAYS show the COST of their choices",
            "What are they AFRAID of? (specific, not abstract)",
            "One internal 'why' moment per chapter",
            "Motivations visible through action + brief internal thought"
          ],
          "question": "What does this choice COST this character? What are they giving up?"
        },
        "tier_2_supporting_characters": {
          "examples": ["Best friend", "Sibling", "Mentor", "Band member", "Rival"],
          "requirements": [
            "ONE defining moment that shows who they are",
            "Reveals why they matter to the protagonist",
            "Specific detail that makes them real, not cardboard"
          ],
          "question": "What is the ONE moment that shows this person's character?"
        },
        "tier_3_background_characters": {
          "examples": ["Teachers", "Parents at PTA", "Customers", "Town gossips"],
          "requirements": [
            "They are FORCES, not people",
            "Show WHAT they do, not WHY",
            "Concrete details (how they look, what they say) but NO internal life"
          ],
          "question": "Does this character's motivation affect the plot? If NO → Tier 3."
        },
        "golden_rule": "If a character makes a choice that AFFECTS THE PLOT → Show WHY (Tier 1 or 2). If a character is BACKDROP or ATMOSPHERE → Show WHAT (Tier 3)."
      },
      "money_sentence_principle": {
        "definition": "4-6 sentences per chapter that 'hit hard' and earn their place",
        "characteristics": [
          "Earn their place through specificity",
          "Concrete details, not abstract philosophy",
          "Emotion earned through action, not explanation",
          "The line readers will underline"
        ],
        "examples": [
          "Classical earned his father's approval. Rock earned his breath. He couldn't have both.",
          "He wasn't playing for Dan's memory anymore. He was playing for the woman who'd stood up to the world and said her son mattered.",
          "The labyrinth has no exit. Fight, compromise, or run—you die regardless."
        ]
      },
      "process": {
        "step_1_assess": [
          "Read chapter through",
          "Identify repetition (words/phrases used 3+ times)",
          "Find the 'money moment' (scene that HAS to work)",
          "Note which characters appear (Tier 1/2/3?)"
        ],
        "step_2_scalpel": [
          "Remove section headers",
          "Cut repetitive phrases",
          "Eliminate purple prose",
          "Remove abstract emotions",
          "Delete over-explaining"
        ],
        "step_3_hose": [
          "Add character's specific fear",
          "Show COST of their choice",
          "Add concrete sensory detail",
          "Craft money sentence",
          "Give Tier 2 characters defining moment"
        ],
        "step_4_polish": [
          "Read aloud (catches awkward rhythm)",
          "Check sentence variety",
          "Verify timeline/consistency",
          "Confirm word count (1,200-1,500)"
        ]
      },
      "success_metrics": {
        "starting_point": "6/10 - 'Solid foundation'",
        "after_scalpel_hose": "8-9/10 - 'Publishable quality'",
        "target": "8-9/10 throughout for publication readiness",
        "quality_benchmarks": [
          "Word count: 1,200-1,500",
          "Repetition eliminated (no word used 3+ times)",
          "Purple prose cut",
          "Tier 1 characters: FEAR and COST visible",
          "Tier 2 characters: ONE defining moment",
          "Money sentence present and earned",
          "Every sentence earns its place",
          "Timeline consistent",
          "Reads smoothly aloud"
        ]
      },
      "philosophical_foundation": [
        "Dual Action - Simultaneous cut & build (efficiency)",
        "Character Depth Hierarchy - Universal framework (eliminates guesswork)",
        "Specific Questions Drive Depth - Not random 'make it better'",
        "Earned Emotion Through Specificity - Concrete details carry meaning",
        "Brutal Honesty Accepted - Agent must acknowledge failures, not defend",
        "User's Voice Preserved - ((comment)) system keeps user's aesthetic",
        "Systematic Application - Methodical, not random inspiration",
        "Trust + Verification - User trusts agent within constraints",
        "Memory Across Chapters - Track patterns, maintain consistency",
        "Focus on Reader Experience - 'Trust the reader' = core principle"
      ]
    },

        {
          "topic": "adverbs",
          "quote": "The road to hell is paved with adverbs.",
          "explanation": "Adverbs often weaken prose. Instead of 'he said loudly,' use 'he shouted' or 'he bellowed.' Strong verbs are more effective than weak verbs with modifiers."
        },
        {
          "topic": "passive_voice",
          "quote": "The passive voice is weak and circuitous.",
          "explanation": "Use active voice for clarity and impact. 'The ball was hit by John' is weaker than 'John hit the ball.'"
        },
        {
          "topic": "character",
          "quote": "Character drives plot, not the other way around.",
          "explanation": "Let your characters' motivations and personalities drive the story. Don't force characters to serve plot; let plot emerge from character."
        },
        {
          "topic": "first_draft",
          "quote": "Write with the door closed, rewrite with the door open.",
          "explanation": "First draft is for you - write freely without judgment. Subsequent drafts are for readers - refine and polish with their experience in mind."
        },
        {
          "topic": "killing_darlings",
          "quote": "Kill your darlings, kill your darlings, even when it breaks your egocentric little scribbler's heart, kill your darlings.",
          "explanation": "Be willing to cut passages you love if they don't serve the story. Attachment to beautiful prose can weaken the overall narrative."
        },
        {
          "topic": "description",
          "quote": "Description begins in the writer's imagination, but should finish in the reader's.",
          "explanation": "Give readers enough detail to picture the scene, but leave room for their imagination. Over-description can distance readers from the story."
        },
        {
          "topic": "dialogue",
          "quote": "Dialogue is a skill best learned by people who enjoy talking and listening to others.",
          "explanation": "Good dialogue comes from observing how people actually speak. Listen to conversations, notice rhythms and patterns, and avoid exposition-heavy dialogue."
        },
        {
          "topic": "themes",
          "quote": "The themes in your work are like the bones in your body - you can't see them, but they hold everything together.",
          "explanation": "Themes should emerge naturally from story and character. Don't force themes; let them develop organically."
        },
        {
          "topic": "fear",
          "quote": "The scariest moment is always just before you start.",
          "explanation": "Beginning a writing project can be intimidating, but the fear diminishes once you begin. Start writing, even if it's imperfect."
        }
      ],
      "style_summary": {
        "prose_style": "Conversational, accessible, working/middle-class. No pretension. Vivid, often visceral metaphors grounded in physical/sensory.",
        "pacing": "Page-turner pacing. Knows when to speed up, slow down.",
        "narrative_technique": "Third person omniscient OR limited, often multiple POVs. Patient setup, then accelerating tension.",
        "character_voice": "Rich backstory. Natural, specific to character. Blue-collar authentic dialogue.",
        "themes": "Evil (supernatural and human), childhood trauma, small-town America, addiction and recovery, ordinary heroism"
      }
    },
    
    "Anne Lamott": {
      "source": "Bird by Bird: Some Instructions on Writing and Life",
      "core_philosophy": "Writing is a messy, imperfect process. Perfectionism is the enemy of creativity.",
      "principles": [
        {
          "topic": "first_draft",
          "quote": "The first draft is the child's draft, where you let it all pour out and then let it romp all over the place.",
          "explanation": "First drafts are meant to be messy. Give yourself permission to write badly in order to discover what you're really trying to say."
        },
        {
          "topic": "perfectionism",
          "quote": "Perfectionism is the voice of the oppressor, the enemy of the people.",
          "explanation": "Striving for perfection in early drafts prevents creativity. Allow yourself to write imperfectly, knowing you can revise later."
        },
        {
          "topic": "short_assignments",
          "quote": "You take it bird by bird.",
          "explanation": "Break overwhelming projects into small, manageable pieces. Focus on one small section at a time rather than the entire work."
        }
      ]
    },
    
    "Brandon Sanderson": {
      "source": "Writing Excuses podcast, lectures, blog posts",
      "core_philosophy": "Writing is a learnable craft. Understand the rules, then break them intentionally.",
      "principles": [
        {
          "topic": "worldbuilding",
          "quote": "Magic systems should have rules and limitations.",
          "explanation": "Internal consistency is crucial. Magic or fantastical elements need clear rules, even if those rules are simple, to maintain reader engagement and suspension of disbelief."
        },
        {
          "topic": "character",
          "quote": "Characters need to want something, even if it's only a glass of water.",
          "explanation": "Every scene needs character motivation. Characters must have clear desires that drive their actions, even in small scenes."
        },
        {
          "topic": "plot",
          "quote": "Plot is what happens. Story is what it means.",
          "explanation": "Plot is the sequence of events. Story is the emotional and thematic journey. Both are necessary, but story is what resonates with readers."
        }
      ]
    }
  },

  "methodologies": {
    "scalpel_hose": {
      "name": "Scalpel & Hose Philosophy",
      "subtitle": "The Blood Brothers System for Novel Writing",
      "source": "Agent_Work/SCALPEL_HOSE_PHILOSOPHY.md",
      "description": "A dual methodology for editing and enhancing prose: Scalpel cuts with precision, Hose adds depth with specificity",
      "core_principle": "Cut 50% + Add Depth = Exponential Quality Improvement",
      "scalpel": {
        "mission": "Cut with precision, eliminate waste, preserve what matters",
        "cuts": [
          "Purple prose and overwrought metaphors",
          "Repetitive phrases and clichéd language",
          "Abstract philosophy and over-explaining",
          "Section headers that break narrative flow",
          "Redundant descriptions and telling what we just showed",
          "Characters who are cardboard cutouts or clichés",
          "Prose that sounds 'literary' rather than authentic"
        ],
        "tools": [
          "Brutal honesty about what doesn't work",
          "Pattern recognition across chapters",
          "Timeline consistency enforcement",
          "Character voice accuracy",
          "Trust the reader principle"
        ]
      },
      "hose": {
        "mission": "Add depth, create meaning, earn emotion through specificity",
        "adds": [
          "Character motivations and the COST of their choices",
          "Specific fears (not abstract worries)",
          "Internal moments showing WHY characters choose what they choose",
          "Concrete sensory details that carry emotion",
          "One 'defining moment' for each supporting character",
          "Money sentences that hit hard and earn their place"
        ],
        "core_question": "What does this choice COST this character? What are they giving up?"
      },
      "character_depth_hierarchy": {
        "tier_1_main_characters": {
          "examples": ["Protagonist", "Antagonist", "Love Interest", "Mentor", "Primary Family Members"],
          "requirements": [
            "ALWAYS show the COST of their choices",
            "What are they AFRAID of? (specific, not abstract)",
            "One internal 'why' moment per chapter",
            "Motivations visible through action + brief internal thought"
          ],
          "question": "What does this choice COST this character? What are they giving up?"
        },
        "tier_2_supporting_characters": {
          "examples": ["Best friend", "Sibling", "Mentor", "Band member", "Rival"],
          "requirements": [
            "ONE defining moment that shows who they are",
            "Reveals why they matter to the protagonist",
            "Specific detail that makes them real, not cardboard"
          ],
          "question": "What is the ONE moment that shows this person's character?"
        },
        "tier_3_background_characters": {
          "examples": ["Teachers", "Parents at PTA", "Customers", "Town gossips"],
          "requirements": [
            "They are FORCES, not people",
            "Show WHAT they do, not WHY",
            "Concrete details (how they look, what they say) but NO internal life"
          ],
          "question": "Does this character's motivation affect the plot? If NO → Tier 3."
        },
        "golden_rule": "If a character makes a choice that AFFECTS THE PLOT → Show WHY (Tier 1 or 2). If a character is BACKDROP or ATMOSPHERE → Show WHAT (Tier 3)."
      },
      "money_sentence_principle": {
        "definition": "4-6 sentences per chapter that 'hit hard' and earn their place",
        "characteristics": [
          "Earn their place through specificity",
          "Concrete details, not abstract philosophy",
          "Emotion earned through action, not explanation",
          "The line readers will underline"
        ],
        "examples": [
          "Classical earned his father's approval. Rock earned his breath. He couldn't have both.",
          "He wasn't playing for Dan's memory anymore. He was playing for the woman who'd stood up to the world and said her son mattered.",
          "The labyrinth has no exit. Fight, compromise, or run—you die regardless."
        ]
      },
      "process": {
        "step_1_assess": [
          "Read chapter through",
          "Identify repetition (words/phrases used 3+ times)",
          "Find the 'money moment' (scene that HAS to work)",
          "Note which characters appear (Tier 1/2/3?)"
        ],
        "step_2_scalpel": [
          "Remove section headers",
          "Cut repetitive phrases",
          "Eliminate purple prose",
          "Remove abstract emotions",
          "Delete over-explaining"
        ],
        "step_3_hose": [
          "Add character's specific fear",
          "Show COST of their choice",
          "Add concrete sensory detail",
          "Craft money sentence",
          "Give Tier 2 characters defining moment"
        ],
        "step_4_polish": [
          "Read aloud (catches awkward rhythm)",
          "Check sentence variety",
          "Verify timeline/consistency",
          "Confirm word count (1,200-1,500)"
        ]
      },
      "success_metrics": {
        "starting_point": "6/10 - 'Solid foundation'",
        "after_scalpel_hose": "8-9/10 - 'Publishable quality'",
        "target": "8-9/10 throughout for publication readiness",
        "quality_benchmarks": [
          "Word count: 1,200-1,500",
          "Repetition eliminated (no word used 3+ times)",
          "Purple prose cut",
          "Tier 1 characters: FEAR and COST visible",
          "Tier 2 characters: ONE defining moment",
          "Money sentence present and earned",
          "Every sentence earns its place",
          "Timeline consistent",
          "Reads smoothly aloud"
        ]
      },
      "philosophical_foundation": [
        "Dual Action - Simultaneous cut & build (efficiency)",
        "Character Depth Hierarchy - Universal framework (eliminates guesswork)",
        "Specific Questions Drive Depth - Not random 'make it better'",
        "Earned Emotion Through Specificity - Concrete details carry meaning",
        "Brutal Honesty Accepted - Agent must acknowledge failures, not defend",
        "User's Voice Preserved - ((comment)) system keeps user's aesthetic",
        "Systematic Application - Methodical, not random inspiration",
        "Trust + Verification - User trusts agent within constraints",
        "Memory Across Chapters - Track patterns, maintain consistency",
        "Focus on Reader Experience - 'Trust the reader' = core principle"
      ]
    },
    
    "compass_advanced_tools": {
      "name": "COMPASS Advanced Cursor Novel Writing Tools",
      "source": "zz_COMPASS/Core/ADVANCED_CURSOR_NOVEL_WRITING_TOOLS.md",
      "description": "Comprehensive system of revolutionary fiction writing technology combining automatic intelligence, professional feedback, and creative control",
      "core_systems": {
        "writing_dna_memory": {
          "description": "Revolutionary automatic memory system that captures the 'genetic code' of your novel",
          "features": [
            "Automatic Plot Capture: Remembers all plot decisions, character relationships, and timeline events",
            "Intelligent Character Tracking: Tracks character development, voice evolution, and consistency",
            "World DNA: Remembers setting details, locations, historical context, and atmospheric elements",
            "Consistency Checks: Real-time validation for plot holes, character consistency, timeline accuracy",
            "Hybrid Alert System: Critical issues trigger real-time alerts, minor issues available on-demand",

          ]
        },
        "specialized_agent_system": {
          "description": "Four professional-grade writing assistants with distinct expertise",
          "agents": [
            "STRUCTURAL_PERFECTION_SPECIALIST: Analyzes pacing, structure, flow, and narrative architecture",
            "CHARACTER_DEPTH_COACH: Focuses on character development, voice consistency, and psychological depth",
            "PROSE_POLISH_EXPERT: Handles sentence-level refinement, rhythm, and prose quality",
            "STRATEGIC_WRITING_COORDINATOR: Strategic oversight, workflow coordination, and quality control"
          ],
          "mode_system": "All agents support Neutral (default), Nice (supportive), and Brutal (professional critique) modes",
          "workflow": "Sequential: Strategic → Structural → Character → Prose for maximum efficiency"
        },
        "cursor_configuration": {
          "components": [
            ".cursor/workspace.json: Project dashboard tracking status and milestones",
            ".cursorrules: AI writing assistant manual with writing values and directives",
            ".cursor/prompts.json: Quick command library for specific writing tasks",
            ".cursor/commands/: Detailed style command files for consistent voice application",
            ".cursorignore: Specifies files/directories that Git and AI should ignore"
          ]
        },
        "json_prompts_system": {
          "description": "Powerful functional commands for specific writing transformations",
          "prompts": [
            "pov_conversion: Convert passages to 3rd person omniscient while maintaining poetic prose style",
            "transition_creation: Create smooth transitions that set up next chapter without duplicating content",
            "class_divide_theme: Enhance class division themes while maintaining authentic character voices",
            "scene_creation: Transform outlines into complete scenes with rich sensory details and emotional depth",
            "inner_dialogue: Enhance inner dialogue to show character thoughts, emotions, and internal conflicts"
          ]
        }
      },
      "advanced_systems": {
        "smart_rewrites": {
          "description": "Context-aware prose enhancement with multiple transformation levels",
          "features": [
            "Liberty Levels: Conservative (minimal changes), Moderate (balanced enhancement), High (dramatic transformation)",
            "Comparison Views: Side-by-side original vs. enhanced text",
            "Agent Integration: Incorporates feedback from specialized agents",
            "POV Conversion: Transform between 1st and 3rd person while maintaining character voice",
            "Creative Pipeline: Brainstorm → Outline → Scene Details → Smart Rewrite → Author Review → Final Polish"
          ]
        },
        "enhanced_breadcrumbs": {
          "description": "Intelligent navigation system with automatic context loading",
          "features": [
            "Current Location: Where you are in the novel structure and timeline",
            "Character Context: Which character's POV and their current emotional/psychological state",
            "Timeline Position: What's happening when and how it connects to broader story",
            "World Context: Relevant setting details, historical background, and atmospheric elements",
            "Plot Threads: Active storylines and their current status",
            "Thematic Elements: Underlying themes being explored and their development"
          ]
        }
      },
      "revolutionary_potential": "The most advanced fiction writing technology ever conceived, combining automatic intelligence, professional feedback, intelligent enhancement, visual storytelling, quality assurance, and creative control"
    },
    
    "json_prompts": {
      "name": "JSON Prompts System",
      "source": ".cursor/config/prompts.json",
      "description": "Quick command library for specific writing tasks and transformations",
      "usage": "Prompts work in both Composer and Command K for maximum flexibility",
      "available_prompts": {
        "character_development": "Help me develop this character with authentic voice and consistent personality traits",
        "dialogue_improvement": "Improve this dialogue to be more natural and character-specific",
        "scene_enhancement": "Enhance this scene with better sensory details and emotional depth",
        "plot_consistency": "Check this section for plot consistency and character motivation",
        "writing_style": "Help me improve the writing style while maintaining my voice",
        "research_accuracy": "Verify the historical accuracy of these 1970s Boston details",
        "pov_conversion": "Convert this passage to 3rd person omniscient while maintaining the poetic prose style",
        "transition_creation": "Create a smooth transition that sets up the next chapter without duplicating its content",
        "class_divide_theme": "Enhance the class division themes while maintaining authentic character voices",
        "scene_creation": "Take this outline and build a complete scene with rich sensory details, authentic dialogue, and emotional depth. Include setting description, character actions, dialogue, and internal thoughts. Make it feel like a real moment from the story.",
        "inner_dialogue": "Enhance the inner dialogue to show character thoughts, emotions, and internal conflicts with authentic voice and psychological depth"
      }
    }
  }
}

Updating the server to handle methodology queries:


```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
COMPASS Writing System - Writing Guides MCP Server
Comprehensive fiction writing reference database with author advice, techniques, and principles
"""

import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any
from mcp.server import Server
from mcp.types import Tool, TextContent
import mcp.server.stdio

# Initialize server
app = Server("compass-writing-guides")

# Data file path
DATA_DIR = Path(__file__).parent / "data"
GUIDES_FILE = DATA_DIR / "writing_guides.json"

def load_guides() -> dict:
    """Load writing guides database from JSON file"""
    if not GUIDES_FILE.exists():
        return {}
    with open(GUIDES_FILE, 'r') as f:
        return json.load(f)

@app.list_tools()
async def list_tools() -> list[Tool]:
    """List available writing guides tools"""
    return [
        Tool(
            name="get_author_advice",
            description="Get writing advice and principles from a specific author (e.g., 'Stephen King', 'Anne Lamott', 'Brandon Sanderson')",
            inputSchema={
                "type": "object",
                "properties": {
                    "author": {
                        "type": "string",
                        "description": "Author name (e.g., 'Stephen King', 'Anne Lamott')"
                    },
                    "topic": {
                        "type": "string",
                        "description": "Optional: Filter by specific topic (e.g., 'adverbs', 'dialogue', 'character')"
                    }
                },
                "required": ["author"]
            }
        ),
        Tool(
            name="get_topic_guidance",
            description="Get comprehensive guidance on a writing topic (e.g., 'dialogue', 'pacing', 'character_development', 'prose_style', 'atmosphere', 'point_of_view', 'show_dont_tell', 'revision')",
            inputSchema={
                "type": "object",
                "properties": {
                    "topic": {
                        "type": "string",
                        "description": "Writing topic name"
                    }
                },
                "required": ["topic"]
            }
        ),
        Tool(
            name="get_author_style",
            description="Get detailed author style guide (prose style, narrative technique, themes, signature moves, use cases). Available authors: Stephen King, Richard Russo, Dennis Lehane, Donna Tartt, J.D. Salinger, Annie Proulx, Cormac McCarthy, Colson Whitehead",
            inputSchema={
                "type": "object",
                "properties": {
                    "author": {
                        "type": "string",
                        "description": "Author name"
                    }
                },
                "required": ["author"]
            }
        ),
        Tool(
            name="get_quote",
            description="Get inspirational writing quotes. Can filter by author or search by keyword",
            inputSchema={
                "type": "object",
                "properties": {
                    "author": {
                        "type": "string",
                        "description": "Optional: Filter by author name"
                    },
                    "keyword": {
                        "type": "string",
                        "description": "Optional: Search for quotes containing keyword"
                    }
                },
                "required": []
            }
        ),
        Tool(
            name="get_technique",
            description="Get techniques for a specific aspect of writing (e.g., 'pacing_techniques', 'dialogue_techniques', 'character_techniques', 'prose_techniques', 'atmosphere_techniques')",
            inputSchema={
                "type": "object",
                "properties": {
                    "technique_type": {
                        "type": "string",
                        "description": "Type of technique (e.g., 'pacing_techniques', 'dialogue_techniques')"
                    }
                },
                "required": ["technique_type"]
            }
        ),
        Tool(
            name="get_genre_guidance",
            description="Get genre-specific writing guidance (e.g., 'literary_fiction', 'crime_mystery', 'horror_supernatural', 'coming_of_age')",
            inputSchema={
                "type": "object",
                "properties": {
                    "genre": {
                        "type": "string",
                        "description": "Genre name"
                    }
                },
                "required": ["genre"]
            }
        ),
        Tool(
            name="search_guides",
            description="Search across all writing guides (authors, topics, quotes, techniques) by keyword",
            inputSchema={
                "type": "object",
                "properties": {
                    "query": {
                        "type": "string",
                        "description": "Search keyword or phrase"
                    }
                },
                "required": ["query"]
            }
        ),
        Tool(
            name="list_authors",
            description="List all available authors with writing advice",
            inputSchema={
                "type": "object",
                "properties": {}
            }
        ),
        Tool(
            name="list_topics",
            description="List all available writing topics",
            inputSchema={
                "type": "object",
                "properties": {}
            }
        ),
        Tool(
            name="get_writing_tip",
            description="Get a quick writing tip on a specific topic or from a specific author",
            inputSchema={
                "type": "object",
                "properties": {
                    "topic": {
                        "type": "string",
                        "description": "Optional: Topic (e.g., 'dialogue', 'pacing', 'character')"
                    },
                    "author": {
                        "type": "string",
                        "description": "Optional: Author name"
                    }
                },
                "required": []
            }
        ),
        Tool(
            name="get_methodology",
            description="Get writing methodology/philosophy guidance (e.g., 'scalpel_hose', 'compass_advanced_tools', 'json_prompts')",
            inputSchema={
                "type": "object",
                "properties": {
                    "methodology": {
                        "type": "string",
                        "description": "Methodology name (e.g., 'scalpel_hose', 'compass_advanced_tools', 'json_prompts')"
                    },
                    "section": {
                        "type": "string",
                        "description": "Optional: Specific section (e.g., 'process', 'character_depth_hierarchy', 'core_systems')"
                    }
                },
                "required": ["methodology"]
            }
        ),
        Tool(
            name="list_methodologies",
            description="List all available writing methodologies and philosophies",
            inputSchema={
                "type": "object",
                "properties": {}
            }
        )
    ]

@app.call_tool()
async def call_tool(name: str, arguments: Any) -> list[TextContent]:
    """Handle tool calls"""
    guides = load_guides()
    
    if not guides:
        return [TextContent(
            type="text",
            text=json.dumps({"error": "Writing guides database not found or empty"}, indent=2)
        )]
    
    if name == "get_author_advice":
        author_name = arguments.get("author", "")
        topic_filter = arguments.get("topic")
        
        authors = guides.get("authors", {})
        if author_name not in authors:
            available = list(authors.keys())
            return [TextContent(
                type="text",
                text=json.dumps({
                    "error": f"Author '{author_name}' not found",
                    "available_authors": available
                }, indent=2)
            )]
        
        author_data = authors[author_name]
        result = {
            "author": author_name,
            "source": author_data.get("source", ""),
            "core_philosophy": author_data.get("core_philosophy", ""),
            "principles": []
        }
        
        principles = author_data.get("principles", [])
        if topic_filter:
            principles = [p for p in principles if topic_filter.lower() in p.get("topic", "").lower()]
            result["filtered_by_topic"] = topic_filter
        
        result["principles"] = principles
        
        return [TextContent(
            type="text",
            text=json.dumps(result, indent=2)
        )]
    
    elif name == "get_topic_guidance":
        topic_name = arguments.get("topic", "")
        
        topics = guides.get("topics", {})
        if topic_name not in topics:
            available = list(topics.keys())
            return [TextContent(
                type="text",
                text=json.dumps({
                    "error": f"Topic '{topic_name}' not found",
                    "available_topics": available
                }, indent=2)
            )]
        
        return [TextContent(
            type="text",
            text=json.dumps({
                "topic": topic_name,
                **topics[topic_name]
            }, indent=2)
        )]
    
    elif name == "get_author_style":
        author_name = arguments.get("author", "")
        
        styles = guides.get("author_styles", {})
        if author_name not in styles:
            available = list(styles.keys())
            return [TextContent(
                type="text",
                text=json.dumps({
                    "error": f"Author style '{author_name}' not found",
                    "available_authors": available
                }, indent=2)
            )]
        
        return [TextContent(
            type="text",
            text=json.dumps({
                "author": author_name,
                **styles[author_name]
            }, indent=2)
        )]
    
    elif name == "get_quote":
        author_filter = arguments.get("author", "")
        keyword = arguments.get("keyword", "")
        
        quotes = guides.get("quotes", {})
        results = []
        
        # Search in specific author quotes
        if author_filter:
            author_quotes = quotes.get(author_filter.lower().replace(" ", "_"), [])
            if keyword:
                author_quotes = [q for q in author_quotes if keyword.lower() in q.get("quote", "").lower() or keyword.lower() in q.get("context", "").lower()]
            results.extend([{**q, "author": author_filter} for q in author_quotes])
        # Search in general quotes
        else:
            general_quotes = quotes.get("general_writing", [])
            if keyword:
                general_quotes = [q for q in general_quotes if keyword.lower() in q.get("quote", "").lower() or keyword.lower() in q.get("context", "").lower()]
            results.extend(general_quotes)
            
            # Also search all author quotes if no author filter
            if not author_filter and not keyword:
                for author_key, author_quotes in quotes.items():
                    if author_key != "general_writing":
                        author_name = author_key.replace("_", " ").title()
                        results.extend([{**q, "author": author_name} for q in author_quotes])
        
        return [TextContent(
            type="text",
            text=json.dumps({
                "quotes": results,
                "count": len(results)
            }, indent=2)
        )]
    
    elif name == "get_technique":
        technique_type = arguments.get("technique_type", "")
        
        techniques = guides.get("techniques", {})
        if technique_type not in techniques:
            available = list(techniques.keys())
            return [TextContent(
                type="text",
                text=json.dumps({
                    "error": f"Technique type '{technique_type}' not found",
                    "available_techniques": available
                }, indent=2)
            )]
        
        return [TextContent(
            type="text",
            text=json.dumps({
                "technique_type": technique_type,
                "techniques": techniques[technique_type]
            }, indent=2)
        )]
    
    elif name == "get_genre_guidance":
        genre_name = arguments.get("genre", "")
        
        genres = guides.get("genre_guidance", {})
        if genre_name not in genres:
            available = list(genres.keys())
            return [TextContent(
                type="text",
                text=json.dumps({
                    "error": f"Genre '{genre_name}' not found",
                    "available_genres": available
                }, indent=2)
            )]
        
        return [TextContent(
            type="text",
            text=json.dumps({
                "genre": genre_name,
                **genres[genre_name]
            }, indent=2)
        )]
    
    elif name == "search_guides":
        query = arguments.get("query", "").lower()
        
        results = {
            "query": query,
            "matches": {
                "authors": [],
                "topics": [],
                "quotes": [],
                "techniques": []
            }
        }
        
        # Search authors
        for author_name, author_data in guides.get("authors", {}).items():
            author_str = json.dumps(author_data).lower()
            if query in author_str:
                results["matches"]["authors"].append(author_name)
        
        # Search topics
        for topic_name, topic_data in guides.get("topics", {}).items():
            topic_str = json.dumps(topic_data).lower()
            if query in topic_str:
                results["matches"]["topics"].append(topic_name)
        
        # Search quotes
        for author_key, quote_list in guides.get("quotes", {}).items():
            for quote_data in quote_list:
                quote_str = json.dumps(quote_data).lower()
                if query in quote_str:
                    results["matches"]["quotes"].append({
                        "quote": quote_data.get("quote", "")[:100] + "...",
                        "author": quote_data.get("author", author_key.replace("_", " ").title())
                    })
        
        # Search techniques
        for tech_type, tech_list in guides.get("techniques", {}).items():
            tech_str = " ".join(tech_list).lower()
            if query in tech_str:
                results["matches"]["techniques"].append(tech_type)
        
        total_matches = sum(len(v) for v in results["matches"].values())
        results["total_matches"] = total_matches
        
        return [TextContent(
            type="text",
            text=json.dumps(results, indent=2)
        )]
    
    elif name == "list_authors":
        authors = guides.get("authors", {})
        author_styles = guides.get("author_styles", {})
        
        all_authors = list(set(list(authors.keys()) + list(author_styles.keys())))
        
        return [TextContent(
            type="text",
            text=json.dumps({
                "authors": sorted(all_authors),
                "count": len(all_authors),
                "with_advice": list(authors.keys()),
                "with_styles": list(author_styles.keys())
            }, indent=2)
        )]
    
    elif name == "list_topics":
        topics = guides.get("topics", {})
        
        return [TextContent(
            type="text",
            text=json.dumps({
                "topics": list(topics.keys()),
                "count": len(topics),
                "descriptions": {k: v.get("description", "") for k, v in topics.items()}
            }, indent=2)
        )]
    
    elif name == "get_writing_tip":
        topic = arguments.get("topic", "")
        author = arguments.get("author", "")
        
        # If author specified, get a principle from that author
        if author:
            authors = guides.get("authors", {})
            if author in authors:
                principles = authors[author].get("principles", [])
                if topic:
                    principles = [p for p in principles if topic.lower() in p.get("topic", "").lower()]
                if principles:
                    selected = principles[0]
                    return [TextContent(
                        type="text",
                        text=json.dumps({
                            "tip": selected.get("quote", ""),
                            "explanation": selected.get("explanation", ""),
                            "author": author,
                            "topic": selected.get("topic", "")
                        }, indent=2)
                    )]
        
        # If topic specified, get guidance from that topic
        if topic:
            topics = guides.get("topics", {})
            if topic in topics:
                topic_data = topics[topic]
                principles = topic_data.get("principles", [])
                if principles:
                    return [TextContent(
                        type="text",
                        text=json.dumps({
                            "tip": principles[0] if isinstance(principles[0], str) else principles[0].get("principle", ""),
                            "topic": topic,
                            "description": topic_data.get("description", "")
                        }, indent=2)
                    )]
        
        # Default: return a general tip
        return [TextContent(
            type="text",
            text=json.dumps({
                "tip": "Read a lot and write a lot. - Stephen King",
                "explanation": "The only way to become a better writer is through consistent practice. Read widely across genres to understand what works, and write regularly to develop your craft.",
                "author": "Stephen King"
            }, indent=2)
        )]
    
    return [TextContent(
        type="text",
        text=json.dumps({"error": f"Unknown tool: {name}"}, indent=2)
    )]

async def main():
    """Run the MCP server"""
    async with mcp.server.stdio.stdio_server() as (read_stream, write_stream):
        await app.run(
            read_stream,
            write_stream,
            app.create_initialization_options()
        )

if __name__ == "__main__":
    import asyncio
    asyncio.run(main())
